## Legend

**Status (tiers — see METHOD.md; annotate with grade A/B/C/D + Q/F/R/C):**
- CORE: high-quality, well-protected business (Q≥4 & F≥4) — own & wait; catalyst optional upside
- CATALYST: near-term re-rate likely (R≥4), floor real — time the catalyst
- WATCH: would be CORE/CATALYST but confidence (C) is low — verify the open questions first
- CANDIDATE: real business but entry/timing/confidence off — track the buy-zone
- QUEUED / QUEUED_HOT / QUEUED_CORE: passed triage, awaiting deep-dive (CORE = quality-without-catalyst)
- PASS: fundamentally sound, fair-priced (not a thesis)
- PARK: moat real but asymmetry thin now — revisit on trigger
- PRICE_KILL: already 2x+ priced in · SECTOR_KILL: defense/commodity/declining
- CAP_KILL: <$20M illiquid or >$1.5B (out of exclusion zone) · CAP_SOFT_KILL: $300M–$1.5B without exceptional asymmetry
- COVERAGE_KILL: >4 analysts · INTEGRITY_KILL: neg equity / going-concern / delisting / serial dilution

**Moat Type:**
- MONOPOLY: sole producer, no substitutes
- DUOPOLY: two suppliers, high switching cost, regulatory moat
- SOLE_SOURCE: design-locked into customer, 12–24 month requalification barrier
- PROCESS: proprietary manufacturing process, difficult to replicate
- REGULATORY: FDA/FAA/NRC/ISO certification, multi-year approval cycle
- CONSUMABLE: recurring replacement cycle, installed-base stickiness
- SCARCITY: supply-constrained, geopolitical protection
- NETWORK: switching cost from user base
- UNKNOWN: signal present, moat not yet classified

reported NI C$6.1M is largely interest income not operating leverage — full operating income requires SEDAR+ filing; (2) ⚠ Organic ARR deceleration risk: 14% Q2'25 → 10% Q4'25 — if organic drops to 6-7%, M&A flywheel requires continuous dilutive capital deployment; (3) Acquisition integration risk: each NHS point solution must cross-sell into existing Trusts — integration failure or cultural friction would stall ARR density; (4) NHS budget pressure: NHS under fiscal pressure → possible procurement slowdown in discretionary modules; (5) GBP/CAD currency risk: ~70-80% revenue likely GBP → GBP weakness hits CAD-reported financials materially; (6) Technology disruption (AI): LOW near-term — NHS IT regulatory compliance, HL7/FHIR certification, DCB standards create high regulatory barriers; AI won't displace certified NHS clinical software quickly; (7) Customer concentration: ~1,300 clients, no single >10% — LOW RISK; (8) Share price overhang: 52-wk high C$14.64 → current C$7.33 (−50%); 2024 equity raises diluted at higher prices — psychologically depressing multiple but may be largely priced in; (9) Intangible amortization distortion: large D&A from acquisition intangibles (C$20.7M gap between Adj.EBITDA C$26.6M and pre-tax C$5.9M) depresses reported earnings; as acquisitions reach full amortization over 5-7yr, reported earnings improve substantially — serial acquirer temporal distortion; (10) Competitive moat pressure: Epic/Cerner/Oracle Health expanding NHS footprint — VHI serves the gap between large EPR platforms and departmental workflow needs; defensible niche near-term but requires monitoring; (11) EBIT transparency: company reports Adj.EBITDA not EBIT in press releases; full income statement requires SEDAR+; (12) Canadian small-cap liquidity: C$440M cap — thin trading vs Nasdaq names; institutional ownership builds slowly. NET VERDICT: Real NHS moat (52% Acute Trust penetration; NHSE framework contracts; 80% GM), fortress balance sheet (C$120M net cash, zero debt), compelling EV/EBITDA (12x) for the quality tier, 68% analyst-implied upside. Key bear: operating profitability is interest-income-dependent (not yet genuine operating leverage), organic ARR must hold ≥10% to sustain M&A compounding thesis. RISK-ADJUSTED ASYMMETRY GRADE B: Strong moat + fortress balance sheet + compelling price vs quality, but not Grade A because clean operating income is near-zero (interest-income dependent) and organic growth deceleration risk. Appropriate sizing: 2-4% position. DEEP-DIVE TRIGGER EVENTS: (a) FY2025 SEDAR+ annual report → verify standalone EBIT, EBIT ex-interest; (b) Q2'26 results → organic ARR metric; (c) next acquisition announcement (C$120M dry powder likely to deploy). BUY TRIGGER: (a) Topicus revised bid at ≥A$2.00 → immediate +21%; (b) FY26 results beat Aug 27 + management guidance restoration → re-rate toward A$2.00-2.47 analyst targets; (c) FY27 ARR growth guidance ≥12% = organic recovery signal. SIZING: 2-3% position maximum given management execution risk and guidance credibility damage. |
